       ---------------------------
       INSCRIBED TO THE BELOVED MEMORY OF MY FATHER.
       "He was a man, take him for all in all I shall not look upon his
       like again"
       Hamlet
       -----------------------------
       PREFACE
       -------
       I owe an apology to the public for venturing to consider
       Shakespeare's 'Hamlet' capable of adaptation and by me and into
       Tamil. In making the attempt I had only two objects mainly in view.
       The first was, that I may help render accessible in however crude a
       form the treasures and beauties of Shakespeare to my sisters and
       brothers of the Tamil land who do not posses the requisite
       knowledge of English to study and appreciate the original. In the
       second place, as one who has devoted the best years of his life to
       an endeavour to resuscitate and elevate the Indian Stage,
       I was prompted by the hope that such adaptations will serve to
       exalt the dramatic idals current in the country. My friedn Mr V. V.
       S. seems to think that adaptation is likely to interest even
       readers to whom the original is not a sealed book. I shall not
       quarrel with him especially if he happens to be right. The
       rendering is what I have called it an adaptation and not a
       translation. Though I have always endeavoured my bets to translate
       as literally as conditions permitted, I have not heistated to
       sacrifice literalness in the rendering of what I conceived to be
       the reproduction of the names of persons and places, allusions and
       allegories and puns and proverbial sayings so as to suit the Tamil
       garb of the rendering.
       I am bound to acknowledge that, in my desire to publish the
       adaptation, I was considerably encouraged by the application of the
       work generously expressed of some of our eminent men such as Sir S.
       Subramanai Iyer and The Hon'ble Mr V. Krishnaswami Iyer.
       I shall rest satisfied if my attempt to render Shakespeare results
       in some measure at least in promoting the objects I had mainly in
       view.
       I must not fail to thanks my friend Mr. V. V. Srinivasan for his
       introduction.
       MADRAS,
       24th November 1908.
       THE AUTHOR.
       -----------------------------------------------------------
       Introduction
       -------
       It was about sixteen years ago that Mr Sambandam began his career
       as an author. Endowed with a fine and fetile imagination the ardent
       and aspiring author would not be content with anything short of
       original dramas. The conception of a plot was as easy as putting
       one and two together. The writing of the drama took as long as the
       physical act of writing down demanded. Scene after scene was
       written off without
       a single word being scored out and one almost wondered whether it
       was a point of pride with the budding author.
       So it was in the good old days. Since then Mr. Sambandam has
       written over two dozen dramas of all degrees of excellence. And it
       would almost seem that it was not till he was tired of being
       original that a few years back he took to translating inot Tamil
       some of the plays of Shakespeare.
       At first sight it does appear to be the reverse of the order of
       development. Literary talent sharpened by exercise in translation
       may be expected to be put later to the more arduous task of
       original production. When however an author after writing original
       dramas, some of which are of no mean quality, deliberately turns to
       devote his time and talents to the comparatively degraded and
       mechanical task of translating
       and adapting dramas written in other languages, one feels inclined
       to exclaim that the author's powers must have becaome exhausted and
       his springs of originality dried up. But it is happily not so. It
       is not every production to which the term original can be extended
       that can rank as a literary performance over a translation be its
       merits however high. Character and quality are not mere matters of
       class or caste and an unpretentious translation may be a more
       valuable contribution to a
       literature and language than a whole library of worthless original
       productions, so called. And when the translation or adaptation is
       that of a Shakespeare and is not a miseable caricature who can
       doubt that the language inot which the translation or adaptation is
       made would be more richly endowed by the effort than by a hundred,
       even tolerably good stories, told in dialogue and divided into
       scenes and acts.
       It is however beyond my present purpose to estimate the exact value
       of the contribution to the Tamil language and literature made by
       Mr. Sambandam in his rendering of Hamlet. My acquaintance with
       Shakespeare is not that of a scholar and my knowledge of the Tamil
       language is less than that of an amateur. My chief object however
       in allowing myself to be induced to write this introduction in
       English, is, to make for the translation now presented to the
       public and thereby for such translations generally, a claim that
       they would abundantly repay perusal even on the part of those who
       are by reason of their education in a position to study and
       appreciate original
       in English. For, the utility of such adaptations to those who are
       unacquainted with the English language and even to those whose
       knowledge of it is not sufficient to enable them to understand the
       finer shades of thought will be readily conceded. It is therefore
       in respect of persons alone to whom the original work of art, in
       all its
       pristine beauty, is accessible and open, that the question will be
       sure to be asked, of what conceivable use to such can these
       translations or adaptations be, of what use the copy, at best of
       doubtful accuracy and merit, to him owns the original painting
       of the artist, itself. An answer to this question in the
       affirmative would be seen to involve necessarily the establishment
       in a measure of the claim of translation to the rank of a separate
       literary art. It seems to me that it is even a fine art and that
       when its true nature and function come to be properly recognised,
       it will be realised how it appeals at once to the refined scholar
       of language and the student of the art of renderings and
       re-presentations.
       To some indeed such a claim is apt to sound as extravagant. But it
       is really not so. It is the function of painting to represent the
       world through the medium of colour so as to body forth the beauty
       in it. So it is the function of music to represent the world
       through the medium of sound and give us a glimpse of the divine
       pulsations in the heart of existence and the infinite harmony of
       it. Even so is it the function of the fine art of translation to
       represent an original production in one language through the
       medium of another language so as to body forth the beauty of the
       original.
       The analogy indeed goes even further. As it is to the person who
       has seen the original of the subject of painting that the painting
       speaks most eloquently, so is it that a good translation appeals
       most, not to a person who has never read the original but to him
       that has perused it a hundred times and lovingly dwelt on each word
       and phrase and on the thousand points of light and shade, of subtle
       suggestion and inexplicable charm.
       It is therefore a mistake to suppose that the translation of a
       literary work in one language into a different language, is, as is
       generally supposed, no better than a copy in colours of an original
       painting. The comparison would, no doubt, be accurate, if the
       medium of reproduction were identical, as it is in the case of the
       illustration. Colour is dead matter, however much life may be
       imparted to it on the canvas by the skill of the artist and being
       dead matter, its identity is preserved whenever and however applied
       as a medium of expression. But it is not so with language. Language
       is a living medium and every language has a distinct individuality
       and character of its own. Hence it is that the translation of a
       work into a different language is claimed to be its re-presentation
       through a different medium and as bearing more the relation of a
       painting to the subject of the painting than that of a copy to its
       original. In the later case, if the original is available, the
       other becomes unnecessary; but in the former case even to the most
       practised student of the beauties of nature, the sight of the
       painting will be calculated to suggest points and aspects which
       escaped the mind in the act of direct perception. So also is it
       with a translation into a foreign language. Far from being useless,
       it is calculated to suggest to the mind points and aspects in the
       original which might have escaped the most refined and cultured
       mind critically studying the original. A sense of the difficulties
       that had to be encountered, and the measure of success achieved, of
       the countless rejections made and the selection that prevailed, of
       the significance attaching to forms of expression, succession of
       sounds, or any given order of words or ideas, of any differences in
       the resultant impressions are produced, - these are only a few of a
       large number, that can be mentioned, of the aspects of a
       translation which enable us to perceive beauties in the original
       which will not be perceptible on ever so close and critical a study
       of the original alone. Let me add that this has been my experience
       and I have no doubt that a large number of those who read this
       adaptation of "Hamlet" by Mr Sambandam will be inclined to agree
       with me.
       Of all plays of Shakespeare, 'Hamlet' is perhaps not by any means
       one of the easiest for purposes of translation and the Tamil
       language is certainly not the most adapted for such translation.
       The vocabulary of Tamil may even be said to be poor in terms of
       descriptive of mental conditions and feelings so abundant in
       "Hamlet" with its numerous soliloquies. The phrasing and wording
       have reached such perfection in the play that the lady really hit
       it off well, who is stated to have exclaimed on reading it, that it
       was wholly made up of proverbs and familiar quotations. Such
       currency of expression is, however, attained mainly by elements
       which are peculiar to each language and are therefore not
       susceptible of translation. In the language of its origin and
       existence, every phrase and every word has a soul, a face and
       features all its own, and lives and moves clothed in its special
       character and with its chosen companions. It often contains a
       hidden figure of speech and carries with it the aroma of its
       previous history. Vicissitudes of fortune affect its strength,
       character and outlook. It contracts many alliances and makes
       numerous friends and enemies in he course of its long life. All
       thesecannot be translated, however accurately the idea underlying
       an expression or the meaning of a word may be; and it is just by
       such elements that the best lines in Hamlet re constituted. Above
       all, it must be remembered that the highest beauty of literary art
       consists not so much in what is said as in what is but barely
       suggested or only remotely hinted at, in a something which is
       indefinable and whose very charm seems to consist in its eluding
       definition. This quality is produced for instance by a succession
       of the same or similar sounds, by the juxtaposition of words, often
       by a mere trick of grammar, occassionally by an unsuspected pun on
       the words or conveyed by the etymology of the words used and other
       devices of a like nature.
       The difficult art of translation would thus be seen not to consist
       in rendering each passage word for word literally, or in loosely
       paraphrasing the original and writing a paragraph for a phrase.
       Accuracy of rendering is indispensable. Beauty of rendering is high
       merit. But a translation may be literally accurate and even
       beautiful but fail to produce on the mind that totality of effect,
       of thought and emotion, which is produced by the original. A
       translation that possess all these qualities is rare to find. I
       consider it therefore a privilege to be permitted to introduce to
       the public, my friend's adaptation of "Hamlet", contained in the
       following pages. There may be faults in it of sorts and kinds. It
       may indeed be capable of improvement here, there and elsewhere. But
       to admit all this, is only to admit that it is the work of a human
       being. All told however and every deduction made, Mr. Sambandam's
       rendering of "Hamlet" would for ever remain a specimen of high
       literary art.
       It revealed to me the possibility of translation attaining to the
       excellence of fine art. It has impressed me with a sense of the
       untried capacity of the Tamil language. It has discovered to me new
       ways of understanding and appreciating Shakespeare. It will serve
       to indicate the direction in which high and useful literary work
       may be done in this country and what is more, it has set up an
       almost ideal standard of excellence for all the workers in the same
       field. And it seems to me that it will for long remain one of the
       most noteworthy contributions to Tamil language and literature in
       modern times.
       I find I have been criticising, theorising, estimating and even
       judging without really intending to do so, and what is perhaps more
       to the point, without the necessary knowledge or right. But it will
       perhaps be remembered that ignorance far from being a
       disqualification in a judge, is a quality favouring originality in
       the pronouncements. And having once assumed the role of a judge, I
       shall not let my own consciousness of my ignorance, very
       inconvenient if a somewhat uncommon feature, to stand in my way.
       Mr. Sambandam, has in his original dramas, not merely refused to be
       bound by the rules of the Sanskrit drama on which Indian dramas are
       mostly modelled but succeeded in demolishing and destroying all the
       well-defined land-marks and boundaries of the region of Tamil
       language and literature by inaugurating a new form of dramatic
       composition and insisting on writing in unconventional prose. But
       in the present venture he has, as the result it would seem of long
       pre-meditation and design, trespassed far beyond the original
       limits of that language and literature and annexed large and
       unexplored tracts to it. It is my conviction that such an audacious
       act of trespass ought not to be allowed to go without being
       adequately punished and that he should be condemned for the rest of
       the natural term of his life, which we pray might be very long, to
       the rigorous task of translating and adapting into Tamil, all the
       remaining plays of Shakespeare.
       VANI VILAS, 25th October, 1908. V. V. SRINIVASAN
       -----------------------------------------------------------

       "amalātityaṉ" allatu
       kurjarattu araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ.

                        mutal aṅkam: mutal kāṭci

       cīlaṉapuram. araṇmaṉaik ketiriluḷḷa ōr kottaḷam.
       pirāṇacīlaṉ kāvali lirukka, maṟṟorupuṟam avaṉai
       nōkkip pāṇataraṉ varukiṟāṉ.
       pā. yār aṅkē?
       pi. appaṭi yaṉṟu, eṉak kurai nīyār: nil aṅkē, uṉṉai veḷiyiṭu.
       (villai vaḷaikkiṟāṉ.)
       pā. vēntar neṭunāḷ vāḻvā rāka!
       pi. pāṇatarā?
       pā. avaṉē.
       pi. uṉ nāḻikaippaṭi vantaṉai mikavum jākkirataiyāy.
       pā. nāḻikai ippoḻutu patiṉaintu āyatu, pirāṇacīlā, paṭukkap pō nī.
       pi. viṭuvittataṟku mikavum vantaṉam. muḷir mikavum koṭumaiyā
       yirukkiṉṟatu,
               maṉamum viyākula maṭaintirukkiṉṟa teṉakku.
       pā. kāvalil ētēṉum kaṇṭaṉaiyō vicēṣam?
       pi. kōṭṭāṉ kīccalum kēṭṭē ṉillai.
       pā. cari, pōyvā. vaḻiyil harihararaiyum māracīlaraiyum kāṇpaiyēl,
               kaṭitilaṉup piṅku, kāval avarkaḷ eṉṉuṭaṉ ceypavarkaḷ.
       pi. atō avarkaḷatu aravam kēṭkiṟateṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.- nil aṅkē! yār
       atu?

       (iruvarum villai vaḷaikkiṟārkaḷ.)
       hariharaṉum, māracīlaṉum varukiṟārkaḷ.
       ha. innāṭṭiṉatu naṇpar
       .
       mā. vēntara tūḻiyar.
       pi. āyiṉ umakku nal iravu ākuka!
       mā. pōrañcā vīraṉē, pōy vā cukamāy: uṉṉai yār viṭuvittatu?
       pi. pāṇataraṉ kāvalirukkiṉṟāṉ, nāṉ varukiṉṟēṉ.
               (pōkiṟāṉ)
       mā. hē! pāṇatārā!
       pā. ām - eṉṉa! hariharar irukkiṉṟārā aṅku?
       ha. avari lorukūṟu.
       pā. vārum, harihararē, vārum, uttama māracīlarē.
       mā. eṉṉa, iṉṟiravu itu maṟupaṭiyum tōṉṟiyatō?
       pā. nāṉ oṉṟum pārkkavillai.
       mā. irumuṟai nām kaṇṭa ip payaṅkaramāṉa tōṟṟam namatu maṉōp
               pirāntiyeṉak kūṟi nampēṉ eṉkiṟār hariharar : ākavē iṉṟiravu
               ivarai, iraviṉ jāmamellām nammuṭaṉ kaṇ koṭṭātu
       kavaṉittiruntu,
               ittōṟṟam maṟupaṭiyum teṉpaṭumāyiṉ nām kaṇṭatu meyyeṉa
               oppukkoṇṭu ataṉuṭaṉ pēcip pārkka, eṉṉuṭaṉ vēṇṭi aḻaittu
               vantēṉ.
       ha. cī! cī! atu tōṟṟātu iṉṟiravu.
       pā. caṟṟuṭkāru mippaṭi, nāṅkaḷ kūṟiyatai nampēṉ eṉum umakku,
               iraṇṭiravāy iṅku nāṅkaḷ kaṇṭatai, iṉṉoru muṟai cevipukak
               kūṟukiṟōm.
       ha. cari, uṭkāruvōm. pāṇatarar itaippaṟṟi kūṟuvataik kēṭpōm.
       pā. nēṟṟiravutāṉ, atō tūrattil, turuvattiṟku mēṟkilirukkum atē
               naṭcattiramāṉatu, ākāyattiṉkaṇ ippoḻutu eriviṭ ṭoḷitarum
               iṭattiṟku vantapoḻutu, māracīlarum, nāṉum, artta jāma maṇi
       aṭikka-
       aruvam tōṟṟukiṉṟatu.
       mā. poṟu, niṟuttu pēccai! atō pār maṟupaṭiyum varukiṉṟatu!
       pā. atē uruvam! maritta makārājāviṉ cāyaiyē!
       mā. nīrtāṉ paṭittavar. harihararē, ataṉuṭaṉ pēcippārum.
       pā. aracaraippōlavē illaiyā? uṟṟuppārum, harihararē.
       ha. avvuruvē. - āccariyamum accamum aṟukkiṉṟatu eṉṉai.
       pā. taṉṉuṭaṉ pēcac colkiṉṟatupōlum.
       mā. harihararē, viṉavippārum.
       ha. nī eṉṉai? maṭintu putaikkappaṭṭa kurjarat taracar cila kāṟkoṇṭa
               aḻakiya pōrk kōlattuṭaṉ in naḷḷiravil turākkiramitta teṉ?
               teyvattiṉmītu āṇai vaittuk kēṭkiṟēṉ, pēcuvāy!
       mā. atu kōpittukkoḷkiṟatu.
       pā. pār, atu otuṅkiviṭukiṉṟatu.
       ha. nil! pēcu, pēcu! āṇaiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ, pēcu!
               (aruvam maṟaikiṉṟatu.)
       mā. maṟaintatu, maṟumoḻi kūṟātu.
       pā. ippoḻutu eṉṉa colkiṟīr harihararē? umatu uṭal naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu,
               mukam veḷuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. itu veṟum cittap
       piramaiyaiviṭa
               atikappaṭṭa taṉṟō? ippoḻutu itaippaṟṟi eṉṉa niṉaikkiṉṟīr?
       ha. cuvamiyē eṉṉetiril vantu kūṟiyapōtilum, itai nāṉ uṇmaiyāka
               eṉ kaṇṇārak kaṇṭirāviṭṭāl nampi yirukkamāṭṭēṉ.
       mā. aracaraippō lillaiyā atu?
       ha. nī uṉṉai ottiruppataippōlavē, atu aracarai ottirukkiṉṟatu.
               pērācaiyuṭaiya pārttipanēcaṉai etirttu yuttam purintapoḻutu
               avar aṇintirunta kavaca matuvē. vivāta moṉṟil veñciṉa
       mūṇṭu,
               pālikanāṭṭiṉaraik kuvalayampaṭak koṉṟapoḻutu, avar puruva
       neḷittu
               nōkkiya nōkkē, eṉṉa āccariyam!
       mā. immātiri itaṟkumuṉ iraṇṭu muṟai, cariyāy naḷ niciyil innērattiṟ
               kellām, pōruk kōlattuṭaṉ kaṭantu ceṉṟār eṅkaḷ kāvalai.
       ha. itaik kuṟittu iṉṉatu eṇṇuvateṉṟu eṉakkut teriyavillai;
               āyiṉum yōcikkumiṭattu, mottattil namatu nāṭṭiṟku
       nēriṭappōkiṉṟa
               ētō ōr peruṅ kēṭṭiṉaik kuṟikkiṉṟa uṟpātam pōlum.
       mā. cari, uṭkāruṅkaḷ caṟṟu, aṟintavar yārēṉum kūṟaṭṭum eṉakku; etaṉ
               poruṭṭu iravellām ik kaṭiṉamāṉa kaṇ koṭṭāk kāval? iravellām
               innāṭṭu ēḻai jaṉaṅkaḷai varutti vēlai vāṅkuvāṉēṉ? pōr mukap
               paṭaiyellām ōr nāḷum viṭātu ceytu varuvatēṉ? āyu
               taṅkaḷai ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikariṭamiruntu tiṉam vāṅkuvatēṉ?
       mīkāmarum
               marakkala vaṉaiñarum ōyvu nāḷ oṉṟumiṉṟi namatu nāvāykaḷai
       yellām
               ēṉ ceppaṉiṭṭu varukiṟārkaḷ, varuva teṉṉām? iravu pakal
       iṭaiviṭātu
               ivvitam viyarkka viyarkka vēkamāy vēlai ceyvāṉēṉ? yār
               eṉakkuc colla muṭiyum?
       ha. colla muṭiyu meṉṉāl, nāṉ ceviyuṟṟa rakasyamum aṅṅaṉamām. caṟṟu
               muṉpāka nām kaṇṇuṟṟa aruvāy maṭinta makīpaṉ, pāñcālaṉ,
       pārttipa
               nēcaṉāl taṉi vempōruk kaḻaikkappaṭṭu, taṉatu
       parākkiramattaik
               kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉum tūyatāṉa pauraṣattāl untappaṭṭavarāy oppuk
               koṇṭa viṣayam nīr aṟintatē. avveñcamaril, ulakattār
       ellōrālum
               cutta vīraṉāy matikkappaṭṭa cūraṉām namatu maṉṉaṉ,
       pārttipanēcaṉaip
               paralōka maṉuppiṉār. jeyittavaṉ tōlvi yaṭaintavaṉ uyiruṭaṉ
       avaṉ
               uṭaittāyirunta tēcattaiyum kaippaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu
       avarkaḷukkuḷ
               ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa, kṣattiriya tarmattiṟkum ottatā yirukkum
       pantaya
               uṭaṉpaṭikkaiyiṉpaṭi, pāñcālaṉ pārellām namataracaṉ
       paṅkāyatu. itaṟ
               ketirāka namataracar takkatōr pākattaip pantayam
       vaittiruntār, pārttipanēcaṉ
               jeyittiruntāl avaṉ paṅkukkup pōyc cērntirukkum.antap pākam,
       avaṉ
               tōṟṟatiṉāl anta uṭaṉpaṭikkaiyiṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭiavaṉ paṅku
       namatu
               aracaṉpāl āṉatupōla. ippoḻutō, pārttipanēcaṉ putalvaṉ
       iḷamaiyiṉ
               koḻumaiyum uṟcākattiṉ cūṭumuṭaiyavaṉāy pāñcālat
       tellaiyiluḷḷa
               kāṉaka naṇṇi, aṅkuḷḷa nītik kaṭaṅkā muraṭṭu vaṉavar
       mutalāṉōrai
               oruṅku cērttu yuttattiṟkuc cittappaṭuttivarukiṉṟāṉām. itu
       namataracāṭciyar
               aṟintirukkiṟapaṭi nammiṭamiruntu taṉ takappaṉ tōṟṟa
       tēcaṅkaḷaip
               palātkāramāy puya valiyāl mīṭkavaṉṟi vēṟoru kāraṇampaṟṟi
       yaṉṟu.
               itutāṉ nammuṭaiya tēcattil ippoḻutu naṭakkum
       āyattaṅkaḷukkum,
               nāmippoḻutu kāva liruppataṟkum, nāṭeṅkum kuḻappamāy
       viraivil
       yuttacaṉṉāka māvataṟkum mukkiyamāṉa kāraṇameṉa niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ.
       pā. ataiyaṉṟi vēṟu kāraṇamillaiyeṉa nāṉ niṉaikkiṟēṉ. nām
       kāvalirukku
               miṭam kavaca maṇintu pōrk kōlattuṭaṉ iv varuvam ic camayam
       varuvatu
               takkatākavē irukkiṉṟatu: inta yuttattiṟ kellām ak kālattum
       ik
               kālattum kāraṇamāya namataracarai ottē yirukkiṉṟatu.
       ha. itu eṉakku mikunta maṉak kavalaiyai uṇṭupaṇṇukiṉṟatu. pūrvam
       oppuyar
               vaṟṟa iṭcavāku vamcat tutitta vaḷḷalākiya irājēntiraṉ
       tilīpaṉ maṭiyuṅ
               kālattiṟkuc caṟṟumuṉ, pūtap pirēta picācaṅka ḷellām pūmi
       yeṅkum
               tiriyat toṭaṅkiṉavām: ākāyattil vāl naṭcattiraṅkaḷum
       viṇmīṉkaḷum
               tōṉṟi utira maḻai poḻintu varappōkiṉṟa uṟpātaṅkaḷai
       uṇarttiyavām,
               camuttira maṇṭalattai aṭakki yāḷum cītak kiraṇamuṭaic
       cantiraṉum
               kirakaṇattāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu yukānta kālatti liruppatupōl
       taṉṉoḷi
               maḻuṅkik kaṟuttaṉaṉām, ittakaiya makōṟ pātaṅkaḷ iṉi varap
       pōkiṉṟa
               peruṅkēṭṭai uṇarttuvatēpōl - piṉ varum keṭutiyai muṉ
       uṇarttuñ
               cakuṉamāka - ākāyamum pūmiyum, namatu nāṭṭiṟkum
       nāṭṭiṉarukkum
               aṟivittirukkiṉṟaṉ.
       aruvam maṟupaṭiyum tōṟṟukiṟatu.
       poṟu! atō pār! atō maṟupaṭi varukiṉṟatu! - etiriṟ celkiṟēṉ, eṉṉai
               aṟaintālum cari! - alakaiyē, nil! uṉṉāl vāy tiṟantu pēca
       muṭiyumāyiṉ
               pēcu eṉṉuṭaṉ : uṉ tuyarattai nīkki eṉakku naṉmai payakkac
       ceyya vēṇṭiya
               naṟkāriyam ētāvatu uṇṭāṉāl, col eṉṉiṭam! - muṉpaṟintu
       vilakka valla,
               uṉ nāṭṭiṟkuc campavikkap pōkiṉṟa kēṭṭai ētēṉum aṟivaiyēl,
               kūṟu eṉṉiṭam! - jaṉaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṟapaṭi, āyuṭkālattil
       aniyāyamāy
               apakarittup putaittu vaitta ētēṉum poruḷiṉporuṭṭu ivvitam
               iṟantapiṉ alakai yurukkoṇṭu tiriyavēṇṭi vantāl, ataippaṟṟi
       urai
               eṉakku! - niṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup pō! - māracīlā, pōka viṭātē
       atai! [kōḻi kūvukiṉṟatu]
       mā. eṉ vāḷāl atai vīcuvatā?
       ha. vīcu, nillāviṭṭāl!
       pā. itō!
       ha. itō! [aruvam maṟaikiṉṟatu]
       mā. maṟaintuviṭṭatu! kāmpīram poruntiya avvuruvai nām kattiyaik
               kaiyiṟkoṇṭu kaṇṭikkappōvatu tavaṟu! vēṭṭa vēḷiyai velvatu
               eṅṅaṉam acāttiyamō, avvāṟē itai vēlla nammā lākātu; nām
       vīṇāy
               veṭṭuvatu viyarttamāṉa pirayattaṉamē!
       pā. cēval kūvumuṉ pēca vāy tiṟantatu.
       ha. ataik kēṭṭavuṭaṉ niyāyastalattiṟku aḻaikkappaṭṭa kuṟṟavāḷiyaip
               pōl tiṭukkiṭṭatu. tiṉakaraṉ varavai etirnōkkit taṉ teḷḷiya
       kurateṭuttuk
               kōḻi kūviya uṭaṉ, iravil āṅkāṅku cañcarikkum pūtap pirēta
       picācaṅka
               ḷellām, appu piritvi tēyu vāyu ākāyameṉṉum pañca pūtaṅkaḷil
               eṅkirunta pōtilum, tattam iruppiṭam pōy uṭaṉē
       cēravēṇṭumeṉak
               kēṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ : ivvuṇmaiyai iṅku tōṟṟiya uruvam
               uṇmaippaṭuttiyatu.
       mā. ām, kōḻi kūppiṭṭavuṭaṉ maṟaintatu. srī kiruṣṇaṉ piṟappu
               koṇṭāṭappaṭum kālam varuṭantōṟum vāykkuṅkāl paṟavaikaḷellām
               iravu muḻuvatum viṭiyu maḷavum pāṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉa cilar
       kūṟukiṉṟaṉar;
               accamayattil pēy picā coṉṟum vēḷiyil nāṭātām, iravellām
               paya maṟṟatām, kirahaṅkaḷ oru keṭutiyum ceyyāvām,
       etteyvamum maṉitarai
               nāṭātām, enta mantiravātiyiṉ uccāṭaṉamum cellātām. attaṉaip
       pari
               cuttamum paraṅ karuṇaiyum kūṭiya paruvamām atu.----
       ha. ām ām, aṅṅaṉam nāṉum kēṭṭatuṇṭu, atai oruvāṟu nampavum
               ceykiṟēṉ. atō pār, ātityaṉ utayamākumuṉ taṉ civanta
       kiraṇaṅkaḷai
               muṉpaṉuppi kiḻakki luḷḷa parvataṅkaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷil taṅki
       yirukkum
               paṉiyiṉaip payantōṭac ceykiṟāṉ.kāval iṉi kalaivōm. nām
               iṉṟiravu kaṇṭatai namatu iḷam irājakumārarukku aṟivippōm;
               eṉmītāṇaippaṭi kūṟukiṉṟēṉ, nammuṭaṉ pēcāta ivvaruvam
       avaruṭaṉ
               pēcum. eṉṉa collukiṟīrkaḷ? umak kitu cammatamtāṉā? itu
       irāja
               kumāraṉiṭattil namatu aṉpaiyum aṟivikkum, kaṭamaikkum
       amainta tākum.
       mā. ām, aṅṅaṉamē ceyvōmeṉa nāṉum vēṇṭukiṟēṉ. iṉṟu kālai
               avarai eṅku culapamāyc cantikkalāmeṉa nāṉ naṉṟā yaṟivēṉ,
       vāruṅkaḷ.
               [pōkiṟārkaḷ.]
               kāṭci muṭikiṟatu.

                    ________________________________________

                  mutal aṅkam : iraṇṭāvatu kāṭci.

       araṇmaṉaiyil kolu maṇṭapam.
       vātyakōṣam; kālatēvaṉ, kaurīmaṇi, amalātityaṉ,pālanēcaṉ, līlātaraṉ,
       vālitamaṇṭalaṉ, karuṇālayaṉ, mantirikaḷ, cēvakarkaḷ puṭaicūḻa
       varukiṉṟaṉar.
       kā. namatu piriyamāṉa cakōtarar iṟantu kālam ciṟitē kaṭanta
       pōtilum,
               ataṉporuṭṭu maṉattil tuyarattai vakittal muṟaimai
       yāyiṭiṉum, namatu
               pirajaikaḷellām cintiya mūkkum aḻuta kaṇṇumāyc
       cintikkavēṇṭiya kāla
               mituvāyiṉum,māṉiṭap pirakirtikku maṉattuya raṭaital cakaja
       māyiṉum,
               vivēkattaik koṇṭu ataṉai veṉṟu, nām iṟantārkkut
       tuyaruṟutaluṭaṉ,
               iruntār kaṭaṉaiyum karutalāṉōm. ataṉporuṭṭē ituvaraiyil
       taṅkai
               muṟaiyāyirunta kaurīmaṇiyait tāramākki, ennāḷum ētila
       rañcum innāṭṭi
               ṉaraciyākkiṉōm. ākavē maḻuṅkiya makiḻcciyuṭaṉum, - aḻuta
       kaṇṇil ānanta
               pāṣpattuṭaṉum, īmac caṭaṅkil iṉpattuṭaṉum, maṇac caṭaṅkil
               maṉattuyaruṭaṉum, iṉpa tuṉapattaic cama eṭaiyāykkoṇṭu. -
       maṇam muṭittu
               maṉaivi koṇṭōm.ivviṣayattiṟku icainta uṅkaḷ iṣṭattait
       taṭṭātu
               ataṉpaṭiyē naṭantu vantōm. itaṉ poruṭṭu aṉaivarukkum
       vantaṉam. piṉ
               varuvatu, nīr aṟintapaṭi pārttipanēcaṉ putalvaṉ nammai
       apalaṉāka avamatittō,
               namatu arumaic cakōtaraṉ maṭintatuṭaṉ kurjarattiṉ makimai
       maḻuṅki aikkiyam
               oḻintateṉa veṇṇiyō, ituvē taṉak kaṉukūlamām camayameṉa
       iccai
               mēliṭṭavaṉāy, taṉ tantai veṟṟi vīraṉām nantamayaṉukku,
       caṭṭattiṉ
               kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉpaṭi iḻanta nāṭu nakaraiyellām mīṭṭum
       koṭuttuviṭa
               vēṇṭumeṉṟu paṉmuṟai kēṭṭaṉuppiyirukkiṉṟāṉ. avvaḷavu avaṉ
               poruṭṭu,- nāmippoḻutu uṅkaḷai varavaḻaittuc cantitta
       vicēṣam ītākum;
               pāñcālaṉ putalvaṉatu viñciya cēṉai mutaliyaṉa avaṉ
       ciṟṟappaṉ nāṭṭil
               tērnteṭukkappaṭṭatāl, iṉi taṉ tamayaṉ kumāraṉukku oru
       utaviyum puriyāmal
               avaṉai aṭakki āḷavēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṇṭippāyt tirumukam itō
       vakuttu
               varaintirukkiṉṟōm avaṉ ciṟṟappaṉukku. avaṉ mutumai mēliṭṭu
               palahīṉaṉāyp paṭukkaiyuṭa ṉiruppatāl icciṟuvaṉatu ceykai
       aṟiyāṉ
               pōlum. ākavē karuṇālayā, vālitamaṇṭalā, uṅkaḷ iruvaraiyum
               innirupattuṭaṉ vayōtikaṉāṉa pāñcālaṉiṭam aṉuppukiṟōm.
               innarupaṅkaḷil vivarittirukkiṉṟa viṣayamaṉṟi ammaṉṉṉuṭaṉ
       vēṟu nīr
               pēci muṭittaṟku umakku uttiravillai. pōy varuvīr, viraintu
       ceṉṟu
               umatu kaṭaimaiyai muṭippīr.
       karu-vā. makārājāviṉ cittam, ematu pākkiyam, iṅṅaṉamē ev
               viṣayattilum eṅkaḷ kaṭaimaiyai niṟaivēṟṟuvōmāka!
       kā. cantēkappaṭavillai atil nām. cantōṣamāyp pōy vāruṅkaḷ.
       [karuṇālayaṉum vālitamaṇṭalaṉum pōkiṟārkaḷ]
       piṟaku - līlātarā, uṉ camācāram eṉṉa? nī ētō nammaik kēṭṭaṉai, eṉṉa
               atu? evaṉēṉum kurjara maṉṉaṉiṭam kuṟaiyiṉaik kūṟiṭa avar
       kātu koṭāta
               tuṇṭō? nāmākak koṭātu nī kēṭkac cutantira millātu, nī
       etaṉai
               vēṇṭakkūṭum? iccaikku aṟivu aṭimaiyā yiruppatiṉum, vāykkuk
       kai
               utavuvatiṉum, kurjara maṉṉaṉ niṉ tantaikkup patiṉmaṭaṅku
       atikamāy
               utavuvāreṉa uṟutiyāy nampu. līlātarā, eṉṉa vēṇṭu muṉakku?
       lī. irātirājaṉē, kēkaya mēkak kēṭkiṉṟēṉ viṭai. aṅkiruntu iṅku nāṉ
               maṉamuvantu uṟṟatu, kaṭamaippaṭi tamatu makuṭāpiṣēkattaik
       kaṇṭu makiḻum
               poruṭṭē. īṇṭu akkaṭaṉ muṭintamaiyāl mīṇṭum anta naṉṉā ṭēka
       eṉ
               maṉam nāṭukiṉṟamaiyāl maṉṉitteṉṉai viṭaiyaḷiyum.
       kā. tantai tantaṉarō viṭai? pālanēcar pukalva teṉṉa?
       pāla. vekuvā yavaṉ vēṇṭa, kēṭṭataṟ kicaintēṉ vāṭṭa maṉattuṭaṉ.
               nāṉum vēṇṭukiṟēṉ ummai, viṭaiyaḷiyu mavaṉukku.
       kā. camayattaik kaiviṭātē, cantōṣamāyp pōyvā. līlātarā,
               teyvam aṉukūlikkumāka! nī kaṟṟa kalaikaḷ kaviṉ peṟak
       karuttiṉ
               vaṇṇaṅ kālaṅ kaḻippaiyāl!
               [līlātaraṉ pōkiṟāṉ.]
       itu niṟka--- namatu ātma pantuvum arumai maintaṉumāṉa \
               amalātityā,---
       ama. [oru puṟamāy] pantutvam eṉṉavō atikantāṉ, paṭcantāṉ kuṟaivu!
       ---
       kā. mēkattāl maṟaippuṇṭatupōl uṉ mukam ēṉ iṉṉum maḻuṅki
               yirukkiṉṟatu?
       ama. avvitam aṉṟu aracē, veyili laṉṟō vētaṉaip paṭukiṉṟēṉ.
       kau. kaṇṇē, amalātityā, ik kaṟuppu vēṣattaik kaḷainteṟi iṉi.
               kurjarattai uṉatu kaṇkaḷāl parivuṭaṉ pārppaiyāl. kavinta
       kaṇṇuṭaṉ
               maṭinta makīparai maṇṇiṟṟēṭi eṉṉa pirayōjaṉam? māṇṭavar
       mīṇṭum
               varuvarō? cātal carva cātāraṇa meṉpatu yī aṟinttē.
       pirakirtiyai nīttup
               parattai yaṭaiya iruppatellām iṟakka vēṇṭu maṉṟō?
       ama. nijamtāṉ ammā, nirampavum cātāraṇamtāṉ.
       kau. āyiṉ itaimāttiram ēṉ oru poruṭṭākap pāvikkiṉṟāy?
       ama. pāvikkiṉṟēṉ! ammā, itu pāvaṉai yaṉṟu. pāvippatu nāṉ aṟiyēṉ! eṉ
               ammaṇi, karumātikaḷ poruṭ ṭaṇiyum ikkaṟuppuṭaiyum,
       kammaluṭaṉ varum
               ipperumūccum, kaṇṇatu coriyum peru veḷḷamum, kānti
       maḻuṅkiya eṉ
               muka vāṭṭamum, ittakaiya kuṟikaḷum kōlaṅkaḷum eṉ
       uḷḷattiluḷḷatai
               uṇmaiyāy uṇarttuvaṉa valla! ivaika ḷellām pāvaṉaiyām.
       oruvaṉ
               ivaikaḷai yellām pāvittu naṭikkalām. veḷiyiṭa ēlāta toṉṟu
               eṉṉuḷḷatti ṉuḷḷē irukkiṉṟatu. ikkōlaṅka ḷellām atu tarikkum
               mēl vēṣameṉa vaṟivīr!
       kā. amalātityā, uṉ aruṅ kuṇattiṟku arukāka aḻakāy
       amaintirukkiṉṟatu,
               uyir tuṟanta uṉ tantaikkut tuyark kaṭaṉ tīrttal. āṉāl nī
       aṟiyāta taṉṟē,
               uṉ tantai taṉ tantai yiḻantār. avar tantai avar tantai
       yiḻantār.
               iṟantavarkkāka iruntavar īmac caṭaṅkeṉa eṇṇi, cila kālam
       tuyaruṟal,
               maintaṉ muṟaimaik kēṟṟa kaṭaimaiyai; itu niṟka, orē
       piṭivātamāy ōyātu
               ēkka muṟal pāpamām. āṇ taṉmaikku amainta taṉṟu, īcaṉ
       vititta viti
               tavaṟeṉak karutic cātippatām, maṉō pala millāmaiyām, maṟṟum
       poṟumai
               yiṉmaiyām, kalvip payiṟci yilā mauṭṭiyamām. piṟappaṉa
       vellām
               iṟattal vēṇṭumeṉa aṟivōm. cātaliṉum cātāraṇam ētum nām
               ivvulakil kaṇṭirōm. iṅṅaṉa mimukka vīṇāy veṟuttu maṉattiṭai
               ventuyar uṟuvatēṉ? cī! itu īcaṉuk kiṉip pākātu. itu
       iṟantavark
               kēṟṟataṉṟu, ulakam uvappa taṉṟu, aṟiviṟku aṇuvaḷavum
       aṭuttataṉṟu,
               toṉṟutoṭṭu tantai yiṟakkat taṉaiyaṉ tuṉpuṟal aṉaivarum
       aṟintatē;
               ulakam uṇṭāki muṉ piṟantavaṉ mutal iṉ ṟiṟantavaṉ varai,
       vititta
               tituveṉa mati eṉṟam kūṟi varukiṉṟatu. ākavē payaṉpaṭā
       ittuyarattaip
               paṟṟaṟa nīttu nammaiyē niṉ tantaiyākk karutuvai. namak
       kaṭuttu nīyē ivvaracuk
               kuriyavaṉ eṉpatai ulakavar aṟika naṉṟāy. oppilāp parivuṭai
       tantai taṉ
               kātaṟ kumaraṉukku evvaḷavu maṉa uvappuṭaṉ kūṟuvaṉō, avvāṟu
       kūṟukiṟōm
               uṉakku. maṟuttum nī pāṭalipuram ceṉṟu kalvi payila virumpal
       namatu
               kōrikkaikku muṟṟilum viruttamākum. ātalāl uṉṉai nām
       vēṇṭukiṟōm,
               ivviṭamē irukka iccippaiyāka, namatu kaṇṇetiril,
       cantōṣamāy, cukamāy,
               namatu ācaik karukaṉāy, namatu mantirālōcaṉait talaivaril
       mutalvaṉāy,
               mantiriyāy, maintaṉāy iruppai!-
       kau. amalātityā, uṉ aṉṉai vēṇṭukōḷai vīṇākkātē; nāṉ
               vēṇṭukiṟēṉ uṉṉai; namatu maruṅkil taṅkiyiruppaiyāka;
       pāṭalipuram
               pōkātē.
       ama. ammā, eṉṉā lāmvarai umatu kaṭṭaḷaiyai niṟaivēṟṟuvēṉ.
       kā. ām, itutāṉ kātaṟ kumaraṉuk kaṭutta kaṉivām uttaram. nampōlavē
               innāṭṭil vāḻvaiyāka! kaurīmaṇi, vā. nām palavantiyātu
       tāṉāka
               amalātityaṉ ivvitam kaṉivuṭaṉ oppiyatu, eṉ hirutayattai
       makiḻvuṟac
               ceykiṟatu. ipperu makiḻccikkup perumaiyāy, ākāyam atirntu
       iṭipōṉ
               muḻaṅki piratitoṉi ceyyumpaṭi, namatu araṇmaṉaiyil muracoli
       muḻaṅka,
               matupāṉattuṭaṉ viḻavu koṇṭāṭuvōm! vā pōvōm.
       (vātyakōṣam; amalatityaṉ tavira maṟṟellōrum pōkiṟārkaḷ)
       ama. hā! aiyō! eṉai varutti vāṭṭum taṭittuk koḻutta ivvuṭalam
       tāṉāyk
               karain turukik kāṟṟuṭaṉ kāṟṟāyk kalakkātō? allatu
       ātyantarahitaṉ
               taṟkolai tavaṟaṉṟeṉa vētānta cāstiraṅkaḷil vitittirukka
       lākātā?
       - īcaṉē! īcaṉē! ivvulakamum ataṉ viḻikaḷum eṉṉa aṟpamāy, āyācamāy,
               aruciyāy, appirayōcaṉamāyt tōṟṟukiṉṟa teṉakku!cī!cī!
       kaḷaipiṭuṅkāta
               vayal keṭṭuk karaṭāvatēpōl pullaṟivum pātakamumē mēliṭṭup
               pāḻākiṉṟatē ippūvulakam! ikkatikkum vantu cērumā? iṟantu
       iraṇṭu mātam!
               illai! iṉṉumiraṇṭumātamum ākavillaiyē! ulakil uyar voppillā
               maṉṉaṉ! avvuttama puruṣaṉaik kaṇṭa kaṇṇāl ivaṉaik kāṇutal
               mataṉaṉaik kaṇṭu mantiyaik kaṇṇuṟṟatākumē! eṉ aṉṉaiyiṭam
       eṉṉa
               kātal vaittiruntār! vataṉattiṉmītu teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟu kaṭintu
       vīciṉum
               kuṉṟuvarē taṉ maṉam.ākāyavāṇiyē! pūmitēviyē! maṟakka
       muṭiyavillaiyē
               eṉ maṉattil! -paci tīrkkap pucippatē appaciyiṉai
       atikarittal pōlum,
               kāṇak kāṇak kāta latikarittuk kaṇamēṉum kaiviṭṭup
       piriyātiruppārkaḷē!
               aṅṅaṉamiruntum-oru tiṅkaḷukkuḷ - aiyō! iccintai eṉṉai
       viṭātō?-
               cañcalamē! uṉṉaiyē stirī eṉpar ulakiṉar! oru ciṟu mātam!-
       kaṇṇīr
               āṟākap perukat taṉ kātalaṉām kaṇavaṉ pirētattiṉpiṉ naṭanta
       nōyum tīra
               nēramillaiyē!- itaṟkuḷ - eṉ tantaiyiṉ tampiyai-nāṉ
       pīmacēṉaṉai
               ottiruppatupōl eṉ tantaiyai ottirukkum, eṉ
       ciṟṟappaṉai!maṇappatā!-
               īcaṉē! pakuttaṟiyum caktiyaṟṟa pacu paṭciyum taṉ pēṭaip
       pirintiruntāl
               pataṟumē palanāḷ, kataṟumē iṉṉum cila kālam!kapaṭamāy aṉ
       ṟukutta
               kaṇṇīr vaṟṟumuṉ kaṭimaṇam purivatō? mayāṉattiṉiṉṟum māṟām
               maṇavaraikku maṉam kūcātu viraivatō? itaṉil nalamoṉṟuḷatō?
       palaṉētum
               payantiṭumō? aiyō! nāṉ vāyait tiṟakka lākātu! uḷḷam veṭittē
               uyir māḷa vēṇṭum!-
       hariharaṉ, māracīlaṉ, pāṇataraṉ varukiṟārkaḷ.
       ha. aracē, cupa muṇṭākuka!
       ama. cantōṣam, vāruṅkaḷ-hariharā!-allatu eṉṉai nāṉ maṟantēṉā?
       ha. illai aracē, hariharaṉām uma tūḻiyaṉē.
       ama. appā, tōḻā, appeyarai uṉṉuṭaṉ māṟṟikkoḷḷat taṭaiyillai.
               hariharā, pāṭalipuram viṭṭu iṅku vantateṉ?- māracīlā,-
       mā. iḷavaracē, namaskāram.
       ama. mikavum cantōṣam vārāy-(pāṇataraṉukku) cutiṉam, aiyā,
       vārum-eṉṉa
               vicēṣampaṟṟi pāṭalipuram viṭṭu vantatu iṅkē?
       ha. kalviyiṟ kavalaiyiṉṟi karantu vantēṉ aracē-
       ama. uṉ pakaiva ṉataip pakarntiṭiṉum ataik kēṭka virumpēṉ. uṉakku
               virōtamāy, nī kūṟuvatai nampumpaṭi eṉ cevikaḷai nī mōcañ
       ceyyalākātu.
               kalviyai veṟuttuk karappava ṉalla nī! cīlaṉapuram nāṭiya
       vicēṣam eṉṉa?
               mīṇṭum pōvataṉmuṉ vēṇṭiyapaṭi kuṭikkak kaṟkalāmiṅku.
       ha. aracē, umatu tantai karumātikaḷaik kāṇa vantēṉ.
       ama. uṭaṉ payilum nēcaṉē, vēṇṭuvēṉ uṉṉai. ēḷaṉam ceyyātē eṉṉai.
               vitavaiyāṉa eṉ tāy vivākattaik kāṇumporuṭṭu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
       ha. ām aracē, ati cīkkirattiṟṟāṉ atu nērntatu.
       ama. cikkiṉam hariharā, cikkiṉam! pālukkuc ceyta paṇiyāram āṟiyatum
               maṅkalamāy maṇa viruntuk kupayōka māyatu! - attiṉam nāṉ
       uyir
               vāḻnttai viṭa iṟantu maṭintu eṉ jaṉmat tuvēṣiyaic
       cuvarkkattil kāṇa
               icaintiruppēṉ! eṉ tantai!-hariharā! eṉ tantaiyai nāṉ
               kāṇkiṟēṉ!-
       ha. ā! eṅku iḷavaracē?
       ama. eṉ maṉatiṉ kaṇ! hariharā.
       ha. oru muṟai nāṉ pārtta tuṇṭu uttamarāṉa maṉṉaṉ.
       ama. maṉṉa ṉirukkaṭṭum, maṉitaṉāy matippōm. evvitam yōcippiṉum
       attakai
               uttamaṉai ottavarai ittaraṇiyil ettiṉam kāṇappōkiṉṟēṉ!
       ha. aracē, nēṟṟiravu umatu tantaiyaik kaṇṭēṉeṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ.
       ama. kaṇṭaṉaiyā? yārai?
       ha. makārājāvai, ummuṭaiya pitāvai!
       ama. makārājāvai! eṉ pitāvai!
       ha. umatu āccariyattaic caṟ ṟaṭakki, ivarkaḷ cāṭciyāka nāṉ
       kaṇṭataik
               kūṟumaḷavum, caṟṟu cevi cāyttuk kēṭka vēṇṭum inta
       vicēṣattai.
       ara. īcaṉmī tāṉai! col cīkkiram, nāṉ kēṭkavēṇṭum.
       ha. iraṇṭumuṟai, māracīlar, pāṇatarar, ākiya ivviruvarum kāval
               kāttirukkaiyil, naṭuniciyil, kāṭāntakārattil, itaik
       kaṇṇuṟṟārkaḷ.
               umatu tantaiyaip pōṉṟa ōr aruvam, talaimutal kālvarai
       kavaca mutaliyaṉa
               vaṇintu, avarkaḷ muṉ tōṉṟi avarkaḷ arukāmaiyil kampīra
       naṭaiyuṭaṉ
               kaṭantu ceṉṟatām.payamu māccaryamumaṭainta ivarkaḷ
       kaṇṭapaṭi, avar kaiyil
               piṭittirunta ceṅkōliṉ tūrattil mummuṟai naṭantaṉarām.
       payattiṉāl naṭu
               naṭuṅkic cilirttuppōṉa ivarkaḷ, vāy tiṟakka muṭiyātu
       avaruṭaṉ oṉṟum
               pēcavillaiyām. ivaikaḷai yellām ivarkaḷ mikavum
       irakaciyamāy
               eṉakkuk kūṟa mūṉṟā miravu nāṉivarkaḷuṭaṉ kāval kāttēṉ.
       aṉṟait
               tiṉamum avarkaḷ kūṟiyapaṭiyē ovvoru vārttaiyaiyum
       meyppittu, atē
               camayattil atē uruvuṭaṉ (av)varuvam tōṉṟiyatu. aracē, umatu
       tantaiyai
               nāṉ aṟivēṉ. avvuruvam umatu pitāvai yottiruntatupōl
               eṉ iru kaikaḷum attaṉai ottilai.
       ama. eṅkē itellām nēriṭṭatu?
       mā. aracē, nāṅkaḷ kāvalirunta kottaḷattiṉmītu.
       ama. nīyum ataṉuṭaṉ pēca villaiyā?
       ha. aracē, nāṉ pēciṉēṉ. āṉāl ataṟkup pati loṉṟum atu kūṟavillai.
               āyiṉum orumuṟai talai tūkki vāy acaittup pēca muyaṉṟatupōl
               tōṉṟiṟṟu. accamayattil viṭiyaṟkālattuk kōḻi uratta
       captattuṭaṉ kūva,
               accaptam kēṭṭatum eṅkaḷ kaṇmuṉ karaintu maṟaintu pōyatu
       viraiviṉil-
       ama. eṉṉa āccariyamā yirukkiṟatu!
       ha. aracē, eṉṉuyirmī tāṇaippaṭi itu uṇmaiyē,-umakkitu terivippatu
               ematu kaṭaṉeṉa veṇṇiṉōm.
       ama. ām ām, nijantāṉ aiyā, āyiiṉum itu eṉakku maṉak kavalaiyai
               yuṇṭupaṇṇukiṉṟatu.-iṉṟaiya kāvaṉ muṟai uṅkaḷuṭaiyatō?
       mā-pā. ām, aracē.
       ama. kavaca maṇintu iruntatākavā kūṟukiṉṟīrkaḷ?
       mā-pā. ām, aracē. kavaca maṇintutāṉ.
       ama. pātāti kēcamvarai?
       mā-pā. aracē, talai mutal kālvarai.
       ama. āṉāl, avar mukattaip pārkkavillai nīṅkaḷ?
       ha. illai pārttōm aracē, taṉmuka mūṭiyai mēlē aṇintiruntār.
       ama. eṉṉa, kōpattuṭaṉā nōkkiṉār?
       ha. kōpattiṉum tukkamē atikamāy viḷaṅkiyatu mukattiṉil.
       ama. mukam veḷut tiruntatō allatu civan tiruntatō?
       ha. illai, mikavum veḷut tiruntatu.
       ama. uṅkaḷai uṟṟup pārttaṉarā?
       ha. ām, caṟṟum māṟāmal.
       ama. aiyō! aṅku nā ṉiruntirukka lākātā!
       ha. umakku mikavum āccariyattai viḷaittirukkum.
       ama. irukkum irukkum. atika nāḻikai taṅkiyatō?
       ha. oruvaṉ cātāraṇamāka nūṟu eṇṇum pariyantam.
       mā-pā. illai illai! iṉṉum atika nēram.
       ha. nāṉ pārtta poḻutalla.
       ama. kēcam narait tiruntatā?-illai?
       ha. uyiruṭa ṉirukkumpoḻutu nāṉ pārtta vaṇṇam iruntatu, kaṟuppum
               veṇmaiyum kalantatāy.
       ama. nā ṉiṉṟiravu kāva lirukkiṟēṉ. oru vēḷai iṉṟiravum ulāvalām
               atu maṟupaṭiyum.
       ha. aṅṅaṉamē ceyyum, nāṉ uttaravātam.
       ama. eṉ tantai yuruvaikkoṇṭu atu tōṟṟumāyiṉ, mauṉamā yirāviṭiṉ
               narakattil vīḻttuvē ṉeṉa namaṉē etirttuk kūṟiṉum, nāṉ
       ataṉuṭaṉ
               pēcuvēṉ. ituvarai irakaciyamāyk kāppāṟṟiya tuṇmaiyāyiṉ,
       iṉṉu
               mappaṭiyē mauṉam pārāṭṭak kēṭṭukkoḷkiṟēṉ uṅkaḷellōraiyum;
               etu naṭantālu miṉṟiravu, maṉattālaṟivataṉṟi vaytiṟantu
       kūṟātīr. nīṅkaḷ
               ceyyum nallutaviyai nāṉ maṟavēṉ eṉṟum; ākavē viṭaipeṟṟuk
               koḷḷuṅkaḷ. patiṉaintu nāḻikaikkellām kottaḷattil vantu
               kāṇkiṟē ṉummai.
       ellōrum. taṅkaḷ kaṭṭaḷai eṅkaḷ kaṭamai.
       ama. uṅkaḷ kātal,-aṉparē, pōy vārīr.
       (amalātityaṉ tavira maṟṟa mūvarum pōkiṟārkaḷ.)
       eṉ tantai yuru taraṇiyil tōṟṟuvatō pōrk kōlattuṭaṉ!-iti lētō
               virukkiṉṟatu! ētō vañcakam naṭantirukka vēṇṭum! iravu
       viraintu vantiṭa
               lākātā? varumaḷavum maṉamē poṟuttiru; pāpac ceyalkaḷ
       pātāḷam
               putaikkappaṭiṉum paran teḻum māntar kaṇmuṉ pulappaṭa!
       (pōkiṟāṉ.)
       kāṭci muṭikiṟatu.

                  mutal aṅkam: mūṉṟāvatu kāṭci.

       pālanēcaṉ vīṭṭil ōr aṟai.
       līlātaraṉum, apalaiyum varukiṟārkaḷ.
       lī. nāvā yēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉ cāmāṉkaḷellām. taṅkaiyē, nāṉ varukiṟēṉ.
               kāṟṟu micaintu kappalum ōṭak kūṭiya kaṭitaṉil varumāṟu
       kaṭitam uṟaṅkātu nīyum
               varaiyaiyāka.
       apa. ataṟkuc cantēkappaṭukiṉṟaṉaiyā?
       lī. inta amalātityaruṭaiya aṉpeṉak kāṭṭum aṟpattai atikamāka
       maṉattiṟ
               pāvikkātē. itaik kātaleṉak karutātē. atai oru mātiriyāka
       matippāy,
               yavvaṉattiṉ viḷaiyāṭṭām. kālattil malarum karuneytal
       kaṭitaṉil
               vāṭik kantam kuṟaivatēpōl kaṇattiṉil māṟum karutteṉa
       niṉaippāy!
               avvaḷavē!
       apa. avvaḷavutāṉā?
       lī. ataiviṭa atikamāy eṇṇātē. iḷam piṟaipōlum aḷam paruvattil
               uṭal māttiram ōṅki vaḷarvataṉṟu; ivvālayam perukuṅkāl
               ataṉuḷ ḷirukkum āṉmāvum apivirtti yaṭaiyu maṉṟō? orukāl
       uṉmītu
               ippoḻutu uṇmaiyil kātalkoṇṭiruppiṉu mirukkalām. ippoḻutu
               avaratu iccaiyiṉ neṟiyil mācu māṟāṭṭaṅkaḷ evvaḷavēṉum illā
       tirukkalām. āyiṉum nī añca vēṇṭiyatoṉ ṟuṇṭu. avar uṉṉata
       stitiyaip paṉṉuṅkāl taṉṉuṭaiya taṉṟu maṉṉaṉ maṉam. taṉ kuṭip
               piṟappiṟkut tāṉ aṭimaippaṭṭava raṉṟō? maṉṉaṉāyp piṟantavaṉ
               maṟṟavaraippōl maṉañ ceṉṟaviṭattu maṉaiviyait tēṭalākātu.
       aracaṉ
               evvitam maṇakkiṉṟāṉō ataṉaip poṟuttatām avaṉatu irājyattiṉ
               kṣēmamum cukamum. tāṉ talaivaṉām taṉ irājāṅkam eṅṅaṉam
               kūṟukiṉṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē taṉ talaiviyaik kaippiṭikka
       vēṇṭiyava(ṉ)
               ṉākiṟāṉ. ākavē amalātityar uṉmītu aṉpu koṇṭiruppatāka
               aṟaintiṭiṉ avaratu stitiyaiyum kuṟitta kāriyattaiyum
       karuti, kūṟiya
               vaṇṇañ ceyya avarāl evvaḷavu kūṭumō, ataṉaḷavē avar moḻiyai
               nī namputal uṉ matikku aḻakām. kurjarattāriṉ cammatiyiṉ
       palamāṉatu,
               avarai evvaḷavu tūram koṇṭu pōkumō, avvaḷavutāṉ avar
               pōkakkūṭum. avaratu pāṭalkaḷukku ati nampikkaiyuṭaṉ cevi
               koṭuppai yāyiṉum, allatu uṉ maṉattai avar kavarntu
       ceṉṟiṭiṉum,
               kaṭṭuppaṭā avar kātaliṉ vēṇṭukōḷukku uṉ kaṟpeṉum pēḻaiyait
               tiṟantiṭiṉum, uṉ māṉattiṟku eṉṉa naṣṭa muṇṭākumeṉṟu nīyē
       cīrtūkkip pār. ataṟkañcuvāy apalā, ataṟkañcuvāy; eṉ arumaic
       cōtariyē, viruppamatiṉ vēkattiṟkum veppattiṟku muṭpaṭātapaṭi, uṉ
       kātalai veku tūrattil aṭakki vai. kaṉavilum piṟaḻāk kaṟpuṭaik
       kaṉṉikaiyum
       cantiraṉ kāṇat taṉ cuntarattai veḷippaṭuttiṉa ḷāyiṉ kaṟpiṉ
       varampiṉaik
       kaṭantavaḷē yāvāḷ. aruntatiyām aruṅ kaṟpuṭaiyavaḷum avatūṟuk
       kāḷākkappaṭukiṟāḷ. paṉmuṟai vacanta kālattil, iḷam puṣpaṅkaḷ naṉṟāy
       malaru muṉṉē koṭuṅ kūṟṟu avaikaḷaik keṭ ṭalaiyac ceykiṟatu.
       iḷam paruvattiṉ utaya kālat tarum paṉinīrai, vaṟṟac ceytu
       vāṭṭivarum
       koṭuṅ kāṟṟukaḷ atika muṇṭu ākaiyāl jākkirataiyā yiru. ciṟanta
       pātukāppu ceṟinta payattiṉi luṇṭu. vēṟoṉ ṟaruki lillāviṭiṉum
       vevviya yavvaṉam taṉakkut tāṉāy āḷukaiyai mīṟum.
       apa. nīr kūṟiya putti matikaḷiṉ cārāmcattai ennāḷum maṟavātu eṉ
       itayattaik
               kākkum kāvālākak koḷkiṟēṉ. āṉāl aṇṇā, karaṭumuraṭām mutti
       mārkkattai māṇavaṉukkuk kāṇpittu, tāṉ taṉ maṉampōṉapaṭi matittu
               mati maṟantu mirutuvām malarpūtta acciṟṟiṉpa mārkkattāl
       aṭaiyuṅkati
               aṟiyātu kāmukaṉāyc cellum kuṇamilāk kuruviṉaippōl, eṉakkoru
               vaḻi kūṟi nī oru vaḻi cellātē.
       lī. ō! appaṭi yoṉṟum payappaṭātē. veku nēram niṉṟu viṭṭēṉ,- itō
               pitā varukiṉṟār.
       pālanēcaṉ varukiṟāṉ.
               irumuṟai āci peṟuvatu iraṭṭippup puṇṇiyamām. iraṇṭām muṟai
               viṭai peṟa it taruṇam vāyttatu eṉ atirṣṭa vacamē.
       pāla. iṉṉum iṅkā? līlātarā! puṟappaṭu, puṟappaṭu, pārttavar
       nakaippār!
               kāṟṟut tākkik kaṟai mukattuk kappal kāttirukkiṉṟatu
       uṉakkāka.
               itō eṉṉāci uṉakkut tuṇaiyām. itō nāṉ kūṟiṭum cila matikaḷai
               maṉattil maṟavātu varaintiṭuvāy. maṉa teṇṇuva tellām
       vāytiṟantu
               veḷiyiṭātē. maṉattil muḻuvatum eṇṇātu ekkarumamun tuṇiyātē.
               aṉyōṉyamā yirukkalām, āṉāl anākarīkamāy naṭavātē. naṉṟāyp
               parīṭcit taṟiyātu naṭpuc ceyyātē; naṭṭapiṉ naṇpaṉaiviṭ
       ṭennāḷum
               akalātē. ārāyn taṟiyātu nērppaṭṭavaṉai yellām nēcaṉeṉak
       karuti
               uṉ nāṇayattaik keṭuttuk koḷḷātē. caccaraviṟ pukuvatil
       jākkirataiyā
               yiru; pukuntapiṉ, piṉvāṅkātē- etiri ēṅkiṭa etirppāy. ellōr
       māṭṭum
               uṉ ceviyiṉaik koṭu; cilarukkē vārttaiyaik koṭuppāy; evar
       kūṟuṅ
               kuṟaiyiṉaiyum ēṟṟukkoḷ. āṉāl uṉ tīrmāṉattai veḷiyiṭātē.
               kai yiruppuk kēṟṟa kalaiyait tari. uḷḷam cellumāṟu
       uṭukkātē.
       āṭamparamā yillātu antastuk kēṟṟatā yirukkavēṇṭum uṭai. ēṉeṉil
               uṭaiyiṉāl uṭuppavaṉai aṟiyum ulakam. atuvum nī pōkum
       pirāṇēca
               purattil periya maṉitaṉ eṉṟāl tārāḷamāyc celavaḻittut
       takunta
       āṭai uṭuppavaṉē! kaṭaṉum vāṅkātē kaṭaṉum koṭātē. kaṭaṉ
               koṭuttaiyēl kaṭaṉ pōvataṉṟi uṭaṉ celvāṉ uṉ nēyaṉum.
       kaṭaṉ vāṅkuvaiyēl uṉ karuttiṉ kūrmai maḻuṅkum. etaṉilum mēlāy
       itai eṇṇuvāy; uṉṟaṉukkē nī uṇmaiyā yiruppāy! aṅṅaṉam
       iruppāyāyiṉ, iravu pakalaip piṉ toṭarvatēpōl, tiṇṇamāy ulakiṉil
       oruvaṉukkum nī poykkamāṭṭāy. eṉ ācīrvātattāl ivai uṉ maṉattil
       uṟaikkumāka! pōyvaruvāy.
       lī. aiyaṉē, viṭai peṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ mikunta vaṇakkattuṭaṉ.
       pāla. kāla māyatu kāttiruppār kāvalāḷikaḷ pō.
       lī. apalā, nāṉ varukiṉṟēṉ. nāṉ kūṟiyatu naṉṟāyk kiyāpaka
       mirukkaṭṭum.
       apa. nī kūṟiya puttimatiyai eṉ maṉateṉum pēḻaiyil vaittup pūṭṭi
               yirukkiṉṟēṉ. ataṉai maṟavā vaṇṇam itaṉ tiṟavukōlai uṉṉiṭamē
               oppuvikkiṉṟēṉ.
       lī. nāṉ pōyvarukiṟēṉ [pōkiṟāṉ]
       pāla. eṉṉa kūṟiṉāṉ apalā, uṉṉiṭam avaṉ?
       apa.oṉṟumillai, ētō iḷavaracar amalātityaraippaṟṟi-
       pāla. ā! naṉṟāyk kiyāpakam vantatu! eṉakkuc coṉṉārkaḷ, cila nāḷāka
       avar
               uṉṉai aṭikkaṭi antaraṅkamāyp pārttu varukiṟārām, nīyum
       aṭṭiyillātu
               tārāḷamāy avaraik kāṇkiṉṟaṉaiyām. appaṭiyirukkumāyiṉ-
       aṅṅaṉam
               nāṉ kēḷvippaṭṭēṉ.-atuvum jākkirataiyāka - nāṉ uṉakkuc colla
               vēṇṭum. uṉṉai naṉṟāy aṟikilai iṉṉum eṉ peṇṇāyataṟkum uṉ
       kauravattiṟkum ēṟṟatu iḵteṉa aṟikilai. uṅkaḷiruvarukkuḷ vicēṣam
               eṉṉa? uraittiṭuvāy uṇmaiyai eṉṉiṭam.
       apa. cila nāḷāka avar eṉmītu kātal koṇṭiruppatākak kāṇpittu
       varukiṟār.
       pāla. kātal! cī! kēṭṭiṉai viḷaikkuṅ kāriyaṅkaḷil pakuttaṟiyum
       aṟivilāp
               pētaiyaip pōl pēcukiṉṟāy nī! avar kātal koṇṭirukkiṉṟār eṉṟu
               kūṟukiṉṟaṉaiyē atai nampukiṉṟaṉaiyā nī?
       apa. eṉṉa eṇṇuvatu nāṉ, eṉṟu eṉakku oṉṟum teriyavillai.
       pāla. cari, nāṉ collukiṟēṉ terintukoḷ. pittaḷaiyaik kaṇṭu poṉṉeṉa
               mayaṅkiṭum pētaiyām ciṟu kuḻantaiyeṉa matippāy uṉṉai.
       mēṉmaiyāy
               matittiṭuvāy uṉṟaṉai. illāviṭiṉ antap paḻamoḻik kēṟṟapaṭi
       uṉ
               peyaraik kāppāṟṟāviṭṭāl, eṉakku oru pēraṉaip peṟṟuk
       koṭuppāy,
               pattiram.
       apa. aṇṇā, avar kauravamāṅ kātal neṟiyilēyē eṉṉai vēṇṭiṉār.
       pāla. ellām neṟitāṉ eṉṟu niṉaikkalām nī. pō, pō.
       apa. coṉṉatumāttira maṉṟu aṇṇā, ataṉai nampumpaṭi ēṟakkuṟaiya ellāt
               teyvaṅkaḷiṉmītum āṇaiyiṭṭuc cattiyam ceytirukkiṟār.
       pāla. āmām, mīṉ piṭikkat tūṇṭi liṭṭatupō lām, mōkantalaik kēṟiṉāl
               maṉitaṉ vāykku vantavitam tārāḷamāyc cattiñ ceyvāṉeṉa nāṉ
               aṟivēṉ. ippimāṇaṅkaḷellām miṉṉalpōl uṣṇattaiviṭa atika
               oḷiyait taruvaṉavām; tōṉṟumuṉ maṟaintiṭum; kātalām aṉaleṉak
               karutātē. itu mutal kaṉṉikaiyām uṉṉai avar aṭikkaṭi
       kāṇaviṭātē.
               avar pēcavēṇṭiṉ, uṉṉaik kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭuvataiviṭa, atikamāy
               vēṇṭumpaṭi, uṉ kauravattai mēṉmaiyāyk kāppāṟṟikkoḷ. itai
               uṟutiyāy nampu, iḷam paruvattavar iḷavaracar amalātityar,
       avar iccai vaḻic
               cella ēṟku mivvulakam; uṉak katu ēlātu. vivarittuk
       kūṟuvāṉēṉ? apalā,
               avar vārttaiyil nampikkai vaittiṭātē. mēlukkuk kāṭṭuvatēpōl
               uḷḷē yirāta veḷi vēṣamām; eḷitiṉil mōcañ ceyyumporuṭṭu
               paricuttamāṉa cattiyaṅkaḷaippōl mēlukkuk kāṭṭum, kaṟpiṉai
       aḻittiṭak
               karutum, vēṇṭukōḷām. muṭiviṉil kūṟuvēṉ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum –
               veḷippaṭaiyāyc collumiṭattu,- itu mutal amalātityaruṭaṉ
               vārttaiyāṭuvatil uṉ kālattil kaṇappoḻutaiyum kaḻittu iḻivu
               paṭuttuvatu eṉakkiṣṭamillai. itai kavaṉippāy, nāṉ uṉakkuk
       kaṭṭaḷai
               yiṭukiṟēṉ. vā pōvōm.
       apa. aṇṇā, kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi naṭakkiṉṟēṉ.
       [pōkiṟārkaḷ]
       kāṭci muṭikiṟatu.
       *************************
                    ________________________________________

                   mutal aṅkam: nāṉkāvatu kāṭci.

       kottaḷam.
       amalātityaṉ, hariharaṉ, māracīlaṉ varukiṟārkaḷ.
       ama. kaṭippatupōl kāṟṟu vīcukiṉṟatu; mikunta kuḷirāy irukkiṉṟatu.
       ha. veṭṭuvatupōl vīcukiṉṟatu vēkamāy ikkāṟṟu.
       ama. eṉṉa nāḻikai ippoḻutu?
       ha. patiṉaintu nāḻikaikkuc caṟṟu kuṟaiyumeṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ.
       mā. illai artta jāma maṇi aṭittuviṭṭatu.
       ha. appaṭiyā? nāṉ ataik kēṭkavillai. āṉāl av varuvam vaḻakkamāyk
               kulavum kālam neruṅkiviṭṭatu.
       (uḷḷē vātya kōṣamum veṭi ōcaiyum kēṭkuṉṟaṉa.)
               aracē! ateṉṉa captam?
       ama. makārājā matupāṉa kēḷikkaiyuṭaṉ kaṇ viḻittu iravaik kaḻittu
       makiḻcci
               koṇṭāṭukiṟār. avar ivvāṟu rēṇu nakarattuc cārāyattaik
               kuṭittiṭuṅkāl, avaratu vārttaiyiṉpaṭi ceytu muṭitta
       jeyattai,
               pērikaiyum caṅkamum ulakattiṟkut terivikkiṉṟaṉa.
       ha. ippaṭi vaḻakka muṇṭā?
       ama. ām ituvum oru ācāramām. āyiṉ nāṉ in nāṭṭil piṟantu
       avvaḻakkattil
               vaḷarntava ṉāyiṉum, eṉakkut terintavarai,
       aṉucarippataṉālaṉṟi
               ivvācāram aṉuṣṭiyāmaiyāl perumai peṟṟuḷatu! intat talaikāl
               aṟiyāt taṭumāṟṟam, nāṉku tikkilumuḷḷa nāṉā tēcattārum
       nammaik
               kuṟṟattiṟ kuṭpaṭuttik kuṟai kūṟac ceykiṉṟatu. kuṭiveṟi
       koṇṭavar eṉak
               kūṟukiṉṟāravar nammaina paṉṟiyeṉap peyariṭṭu pala
       kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum
               pāḻākkukiṉṟaṉar. nām uḻaṉṟu īṭēṟacceyyum uṉṉatamām
               muyaṟcikaḷiṉiṉṟum, namak kurittām nammuḷḷirukkum uttama
       kuṇattai, aṟavē
               aḻikkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē cila māntarkaḷukkuc caṅkaṭam
       campavikkiṉṟatu.
               palkāl, cupāvattil uṇṭāṉa acuttamām maruḷ virttiyaṭaintu
       niṟattai
               māṟacceytu matiyiṉ matilkaḷaiyum araṇkaḷaiyum maṭittut
       taḷḷukiṉṟatu;
               kuṭip piṟappaik karutuvōm, itu oruvaṉatu kuṟṟamallavē, eṅku
               uṟpavippōm eṉpatu oruvaṉ iccai vaḻiyaṉṟē; allatu, mēlukkuc
               cariyeṉak kāṭṭum naṭavaṭikkaikaḷiṉ rūpattai vistarittu
       vēṟākak kāṭṭum
               yātā moru vaḻakkattiṉāl,-cupāvattil vanta mīppōrvaiyō,
       allatu,
               atirṣṭa vacattiṉ maccamō, yātēṉumōr kuṟaiyiṉ kuṟiyait
               tammēṟkoṇṭu naṭakkum immāntarkaḷ-maṟṟa kuṇaṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷatu
               karuṇaiyaippōl māntar vakikkak kūṟiyapaṭi kaṟaiyiṉṟiyum
       kaḷaṅkamiṉṟiyum
               iruppiṉum, anta oru kuṟṟattiṉāl ulakattōrāl tūṟṟappaṭṭu
       aḻivai
               aṭaikiṉṟaṉar; nāḻikai cārāyam ellā naṟporuḷkaḷaiyum keṭuttu
       taṉakkē
               keṭṭa peyaraiyum koṇṭu varukiṉṟatu.
       aruvam tōṟṟukiṟatu.
       ha. aracē! atō pārum atu varukiṉṟatu!
       ama. īcaṉē! karuṇaik kaṭavuḷē! emaik kāttaruḷum! nī kṣēmam
       viḷaikkum
               pūtamāyiṉu mākuka! keṭṭaḻinta pirētap picāca māyiṉu mākuka!
       viṇṇiṉ
               ṟiḻintālu moṉṟē, vevviya narakiṉiṉṟum vantālum moṉṟē! tuṉpa
               miḻaikkat tōṉṟiṉālum cari! naṉmai payakka nāṭiṉālum cari!
       kēvalam
               ikkōlattil uṉṉaik kaṇṭapiṉ uṉṉuṭaṉ pēciyē tīravēṇṭum nāṉa.
               amalātararē! irājāti rājaṉē! entaiyē! kurjaramaṉṉā, eṉa
               aḻaikkiṉṟēṉ! eṉakku viṭai yaḷiyum, viṭai yaḷiyum!
       aṟiyāmaiyāl
               uḷḷam veṭittu aḻiya viṭātīr! pūmiyil putaikkappaṭṭa umatu
       uṭal
               ataip piḷantukoṇṭu ēṉ veḷi vantatu? īmac caṭaṅkuṭaṉ
       iṭukāṭṭil
               camāti niṣṭaiyil, amaitiyuṭaṉ vaikkappaṭṭa umatu pirētam
       pūtalam
               viṭṭu ivvāṟu veḷivantu uḻalvā ṉēṉ? maṭinta umatu uṭal,
       maṟupaṭiyum,
               muṟṟilum kavaca maṇintu, naḷḷiruḷil kaṇṭōr naṭuṅkumpaṭi
       naṭamāṭuvāṉēṉ?
               eṅkaḷ maṉattuk keṭṭā māykaiyāl, pētai māntarkaḷākiya nāṅkaḷ
               maruṭci yaṭaivā ṉēṉ? kūṟum! itu ēṉ? etaṉ poruṭṭu? eṉṉa
               ceyyavēṇṭum nāṅkaḷ?
       (aruvam amalātityaṉai aḻaikkiṉṟatu.)
       ha. uṭaṉ nīr pōka aḻaikkiṉṟatu ummai, ummuṭaṉ taṉiyāy uraittiṭa
               ētō virumpuvatēpōl!
       mā. evvaḷavu mariyātaiyā yaḻaikkiṉṟa tummai taṉiyāy vēṟiṭam
       taṉṉuṭaṉ ēka!
               āyiṉum pōkātīr ataṉuṭaṉ.
       ha. vēṇṭām, pōka lākātu.
       ama. atu pēcātirukkiṉṟatē; nāṉ pōkattāṉ vēṇṭum.
       ha. vēṇṭām aracē.
       ama. ēṉ, itil payameṉṉa? eḷḷaḷavum eṉṉuyirai eṇṇiya vaṉaṉṟu nāṉ;
               ataṉaippōlavē eṉṟum aḻiyā eṉ āṉmāvai atu eṉ ceyyakkūṭum?
               maṟupaṭiyum kaikāṭṭi aḻaikkiṉṟa teṉṉai! nāṉ piṉ toṭarvēṉ
       atai!
       ha. aracē, oruvēḷai ummai akaḻiyiṉ arukil aḻaittuc ceṉṟāl? allatu
               camuttirak karaiyōram ceṅkuttāy niṟkum ammalaic
       cikarattarukil aḻaittuc
               ceṉṟu, piṟaku ivvuruvai māṟṟi, kōramāṉa vēṟoru rūpam koṇṭu,
               ummai mayaṅkiṭac ceytu umatu matiyai māṟṟiṉāl? atai
       yōcittuppārum:
               avvuyarnta ucciyil niṉṟu kaṭaliṉ alaikaḷ atika tūram kīḻē
       mōtukiṉṟa
               captattaik kēṭṭalē evaṉuṭaiya maṉattaiyum kalakki, kāraṇam
               vēṟiṉṟi, kaṭuñ ceyalkaḷaik karutiṭac ceykiṟatu.
       ama. iṉṉum aḻaikkiṉṟa teṉṉai.-pōvīr; varukiṉṟēṉ piṉpāka!
       mā. aracē, nīr pōkakkūṭātu.
       ama. eṭu uṉ kaiyai!
       ha. colvataik kēḷum; nīr pōkakkūṭātu.
       ama. eṉ viti yaḻaikkiṉṟatu, eṉatu ovvoru avayavattiṟkum vāyuviṉ
               maintaṉ valiyiṉum patiṉmaṭaṅku palattaik koṭukkiṉṟatu!
       iṉṉum
               aḻaikkiṉṟatu eṉṉai. eṭuṅkaḷ kaiyai! illāviṭil īcaṉ mī
       tāṇaippaṭi
               eṉṉait taṭuppavaṉai iṭcaṇam koṉṟu aruva mākkuvēṉ!
               viṭuṅka ḷeṉṉai!-pōm; itō piṉ toṭarukiṉṟēṉ!
       (aruvamum amalātityaṉum pōkiṟārkaḷ)
       ha. aiyō! pittam atikarittup piramai koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟār!
       mā. nāmum pōvōm; ivviṣayattil avar colluk kaṭaṅki naṭappatu
               niyāyamaṉṟu.
       ha. vā pōvōm. aiyō! itu eṅṅaṉam muṭiyappōkiṉṟatō?
       mā. kurjarattil ētō keṭṭuppō yirukkiṉṟatu.
       ha. cuvāmi yirukkiṉṟār carippaṭutta!
       mā. appaṭi yalla, nāmum pōvōm.
       (iruvarum pōkiṟārkaḷ.)
       kāṭci muṭikiṟatu.
       -----------------------
                    ________________________________________

                     mutal aṅkam: aintāvatu kāṭci.

       kottaḷattil otukkamāṉa maṟṟoru puṟam.
       aruvamum, amalātityaṉum varukiṟārkaḷ.
       ama. iṉṉum eṅkē aḻaittuc celkiṟāy? kūṟu; itaik kaṭantu vārēṉ.
       aru. kavaṉittuk kēḷ.
       ama. appaṭiyē ākaṭṭum.
       aru. emavātaṉaippaṭa erivāy narakiṟku nāṉ ēkavēṇṭiya kālam
       vantuviṭṭatu.
       ama. antō! pētai aruvamē!
       aru. paritāpappaṭuvatu irukkaṭṭum, kavaṉittuk kēṭyaiyāka nāṉ
               veḷiyiṭappōkiṟatai!
       ama. collum; nāṉ kēṭkak kaṭavēṉ.
       aru. aṅṅaṉamē paḻi tīrkkakkaṭavāy, kēṭṭapiṉ!
       ama. eṉṉa?
       aru. nāṉ uṉ tantaiyiṉ aruvam. iraviṟ ciṟitupoḻutu iṅku naṭamāṭi,
               pakaṟpoḻu tellām paṭuvāy narakil nāṉ pāriṉiṟ ceyta
       pāpaṅkaḷellām
               pōyp paricuttamā maḷavum, vētaṉaippaṭa ciṭcikkappaṭ
       ṭirukkiṟēṉ. nāṉ
               ciṟaippaṭṭirukkum paralōka rakaciyaṅkaḷaip pāriṉi lirukku
       muṉakkup
               pakaralākātu. illāviṭiṉ avaṟṟuḷ ciṟitu pakarntiṭṭālum nī
       maṉa maruṇṭu,
               mati kalaṅki, muḷḷam paṉṟiyiṉ muṭkaḷaippōl mayirk
       kucceṟintu
               mayirkkāl tōṟum viyarttu, uṭal muḻutum kuḷirntu
       tampittuppōy, uṉ
               iru kaṇkaḷum piramaiyiṉāl pituṅkiṭac ceyvēṉ. uṭaluṭa
       ṉirukkum uṉatu
               cevikku uraittiṭa lākātu nāṉ paramām ivvirakaciyam. kēḷ
       kēḷ! nāṉ
               kūṟuvataik kēḷ!-uṉ tantaimītu kātal eppoḻutāvatu
       uṭaiyavaṉāyiṉ-
       ama. aiyō, jakatīcaṉē!
       aru. paḻi vāṅkuvāy! avarai mōca vaḻiyil atarmamāyk koṉṟa kolaiyai!
       ama. kolaiyā?
       aru. veṟuṅ kolaiyāyiṉum koṭiyatēyām, ituvō maṉattukkeṭṭā
               māpātakamām mōcak kolaiyām!
       ama. viraiviṟ kūṟu mataṉai eṉakku, kātalar karuttaippōl kaṭintu
       maṉō
               vēkamāy viraintōṭi oru kṣaṇap poḻutiṟ paḻivāṅkukiṉṟēṉ nāṉ!
       aru. cari! nī takkavaṉē, aṟintēṉ! itaik kēṭṭum maṉattil vekuṇṭeḻā
               tiruppaiyāyiṉ uṉṉiṉum jaṭamāṉa jaṉmam im maṇṇulaki
       lillaiyeṉa
               matittiruppēṉ. itō, amalātityā, kēḷ; nantavaṉattil
       paṭuttuṟaṅkum
               poḻutu ōr nākam tīṇṭi nāṉ iṟantatāka vallavō veḷiyil
       vārttai
               vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu; intap poyyaic cevi koṇṭē kurjara
               tēcattavarellām mōcam pōyiṉar nāṉ maṭinta mātiriyaippaṟṟi;
       uttama
               kumarā! uṉ tantaiyaik koṉṟa aravam avar muṭiyiṉait
       tarittirukkiṉṟa
               tippoḻuteṉa aṟivāy!
       ama. hā! appoḻutē niṉaittēṉ, niṉaittēṉ! eṉ ciṟiya tantai!-
       aru. ām, am muṟai keṭṭa vipacārak kaḻutai! mēlukku mikavum
               kaṟpuṭaiyavaḷpōl miṉukkiya eṉṉaraciyai mōca vārttaiyāṭi,
       pātakamām
               paricukaḷaik koṭuttu matiyai mayakkit taṉ mōka
       valaikkuḷakappaṭac
               ceytāṉ! hā! amalātityā, eṉṉa katikku iḻintāḷ! kaṭimaṇam
       purinta
               kālai ceyta cattiyattiṉiṉṟum kaṇamum tavaṟātu mēṉmaiyām
       kātal
               koṇṭirunta eṉṉaiviṭṭu, eṉakkumuṉ aṟpat turumpai yotta
               appāpiyiṉmītu ācaippaṭṭaṉaḷē! kaṟpuṭaiya stirīyiṉ maṉam
       tēvarkaḷ
               vantiṭiṉum eppaṭik kalakkappaṭātō, avvaṇṇamē vipacāri vēṉil
               nātaṉukkē vāḻkkaippaṭṭu, paramām pañcaṇaiyālum, pāḻukkiṭaṅ
               koṭuppāḷ! poṟu, viṭivatupōlum vācaṉai varukiṟatu!-viraivil
       kūṟi
               muṭikkiṉṟēṉ nāṉ. nantavaṉattil nāṉ, eṉ vaḻakkappaṭi
       mattiyāṉa kālattil
               kavalaiyiṉṟi nittirai ceyyumpoḻutu, uṉ ciṟṟappaṉ oruvaru
       maṟiyātapaṭi
               vantu, vevviya viṣa*ttai oru kiṇṇattiṟ koṇarntu eṉ ceviyiṉ
               puraikkuḷ vārkka, appāḻum viṣamāṉatu pātaracattaippōl eṉ
       uṭal
               muḻutum paravi eṉ tēkattilirunta cutta irattattai yellām
       kṣaṇappoḻutil
               puraiyiṭṭa pālaippōl muṟittuk keṭuttu, eṉ aḻakiya
       mēṉiyellām
               kuṣṭam pōṉṟa tēmal paṭaiyoṉṟaip paravacceytu aḻittatu!
       ivvitam
               eṉ tampiyiṉ karattāl, uṟaṅkumpoḻutu, eṉ āviyaiyum eṉ
       aracaiyum,
               eṉ araciyaiyum, annoṭiyil iḻantēṉ. nāṉiḻaitta pāpaṅkaḷellām
               pariharittup pōkum vaḻikku, puṇṇiya karumaṅkaḷ ceytu
       muṭikku muṉṉam,
               paralōka maṉuppappaṭṭēṉ pāviyāl. amalātityā! pātakam!
       pātakam! akōra
               pātakam! uṉṉiṭamu māṉiṭap pirakirti aṇuvaḷavēṉu
       mirukkumāyiṉ, itaṉaip
               poṟuttirātē! ciṟappuṟṟa kurjaratēyattu irāja cayaṉattil uṉ
       ciṟṟappaṉām
               vipacāraṉ vīṟṟirukka viṭātē!- nī evvitattil ivvēlaiyai
       muṭikka
               muyaṉṟapōtilum uṉ maṉattai maḻuṅkaviṭātē; aṉṟiyum uṉ
       aṉṉaikkuk
               kēṭu cūḻak karutātē! īca ṉirukkiṉṟār avaḷait taṇṭikka,
       aṉṟiyum avaḷatu
               kuṟṟamām muṭkaḷē avaḷatu neñcattiṟ kutti varuttum. nāṉ pōy
               varukiṉṟēṉ viraivil! miṉmiṉiyiṉ kānti kuṟaivatiṉāl
       aruṇōtaya kālam
               āyviṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. varukiṉṟēṉ! varukiṉṟēṉ!
       varukiṉṟēṉ!
               maṟavātē eṉṉai!
       (maṟaikiṉṟatu)
       ama. ākāyavāṇiyē! pūmitēviyē! iṉṉum eṉṉa? pāḻ narattaiyum cērttuk
               kūṟuvatā?-cī! hirutayamē poṟu! poṟu! uṭalamē! kṣaṇappoḻutil
               taḷarviṉai eyyātu uṟamāyt tāṅkuvā yeṉṉai!- uṉṉai maṟappa
               tāvatu! aiyō, pētai aruvamē! alaṅkōlamāyk kalakkappaṭṭa eṉ
       maṉattil
               kiyāpaka cakti neṉpatu ciṟitēṉu mirukka maḷavum maṟappēṉō?-
       maṟappātāva
               tuṉṉai! - ām, iṭcaṇa mutal eṉ maṉateṉṉum pavakaiyiṉiṉṟum,
       itu
               varaiyil nāṉ puttakaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum kaṟṟa puttimatikaḷaiyum,
       ellā
               uruvaṅkaḷaiyum, iḷamaiyil nāṉ kaṇṭatum kēṭṭatumāṉa ellā
       aṟpa

               puttakattil maṟṟeṉ ṉeṟiyuṭaṉum kalakkappaṭṭut tāḻ vaṭaiyātu
       uṉ
               kaṭṭaḷai yoṉṟē varaiyappaṭṭu niṟkum. itu catyam! īcaṉmī
       tāṇaippaṭi! –
               kēṭu keṭṭa pātaki!-pātakā! pātakā! puṉcirippuṭai koṭiya
       pātakā! eṉ
               puttakatti litai eḻutivaittal nalam. puṉṉakai mukattiṉiṟ
       poṉṟā
               tiruppiṉum maṉattiṉi lavaṉē māpātakaṉā yirukkalākum!
       eppaṭiyum
               kurjarattilāvatu appaṭi yirukkkūṭum eṉpataṟku aiyamillai.
       (eḻutik
               koṇṭē) ciṟṟappā! itō irukkiṉṟāy nī! nāṉ maṟavā moḻiyeṉ?
               'varukiṟēṉ, varukiṟēṉ! maṟavātē eṉṉai.' nāṉ catyam cey

       pēcātu,
               oruvarai yoruvar vaṇaṅkip pirivōmiṉi. nīṅkaḷ, umatu
       vēlaiyiṉpaṭiyum

       ēḻmaiyuṭaiya
               nāṉ-um-pōy īcaṉai vaḻi paṭukiṉṟēṉ.
       ha. iteṉṉa aracē, ivaikaḷellām payaṉaṟṟa piramaiyuṭaiya moḻikaḷ
               pōlum.
       ama. eṉ moḻikaḷ ummai varuttuvatu eṉakku maṉak kuṟaiyāyirukkiṉṟatu;
               ām, uṇmaiyil.
       ha. iti leṉṉa varuttam aracē?
       ama. illai, hariharā! irukkiṉṟatu! murukaṉ āṇai, nijamāy
       irukkiṉṟatu!
               nirampa varuttam.- iṅku tōṟṟiya ivvaruvaippaṟṟi,atu meyyē
       kūṟukiṉṟatu;
               atil cantēkamillai. avvaḷavu kūṟaṭṭum nāṉ umakku:
       emmiruvarukkuḷ
               naṭanta viṣayattai yaṟiya virumpum umatu ācaiyai
       evvitamāyiṉum aṭakkik
               koḷḷuṅkaḷ. ākavē, eṉ naṟṟōḻarkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ eṉ naṇparkaḷ,
               kaṟṟaṟintavarkaḷ, vīrarkaḷ, ākavē eṉakkoru eḷiya vara
       maḷikkavēṇṭum.
       ha. eṉṉa atu? aracē, appaṭiyē ākaṭṭum.
       ama. iṉṟiravu kaṇṭatai eṉṟum veḷiyiṭātīr.
       ha-mā. em maracē, eṉṟum veḷiyiṭuva tillai.
       ama. appaṭiyaṉṟu, cattiyañ ceyyuṅkaḷ.
       ha. uṇmaiyāka nāṉ veḷiyiṭuvatillai.
       mā. nāṉum veḷiyiṭuva tillai, cattiya māka.
       ama. eṉṉuṭaiya vāḷiṉ mītu cattiyañ ceyyuṅkaḷ.
       mā. nāṅkaḷ ippoḻututāṉ cattiyañ ceytōm.
       ama. appaṭiyaṉṟu, eṉṉuṭaiya vāḷiṉmītu.
       aru. (kīḻiruntu) cattiyam ceyyuṅkaḷ!
       ama. ā! hā! payalē! appaṭiyā colkiṟāy! aṅkā irukkiṉṟāy
               nāṇayakkārā?- vāruṅkaḷ- nilavaṟaiyilirukkum ivaṉ kural
       kēṭkiṉṟatā
               umakku? - cattiyam ceyya oppuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ.
       ha. eppaṭi cattiyam ceyyavēṇṭumō collum, aracē.
       ama. kaṇṭatai iṉṟu, kūṟuvatillai eṉṟum, eṉa, eṉ uṭaivāḷiṉ mītu
       cattiyam
               ceyyuṅkaḷ.
       aru. (kīḻiruntu) cattiyam ceyyuṅkaḷ!
       ama. appaṭiyā camācāram? iṅkum vantaṉaiyā? āṉāl ivviṭam viṭṭup
               peyarvōm. ippaṭi vāruṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷē, uṅkaḷ karattai eṉ
               kattiyiṉmītu vaittu, kēṭṭataiyeṉṟum kūṟuvatillaiyeṉṟu
       cattiyam
               ceyyuṅkaḷ.
       aru. (kīḻiruntu) cattiyañ ceyyuṅkaḷ!
       ama. naṉṟāyc colliṉai, kiḻap peruccāḷiyē! pūmiyiṉ kīḻ avvaḷavu
               turitamāyt tuḷaikka muṭikiṉṟatō uṉṉāl? tērnta āḷtāṉ. iṉṉum
               caṟṟu appuṟam pōvōm vāruṅkaḷ, tōḻarkaḷē.
       ha. eṉṉa iravu pakal! itu putumaiyām vintaiyē!
       ama. putitāy varuvataip putitā yaṟintu nal varavu aḻaippāyāka!
               ikalōkattilum paralōkattilum uṉatu vētānta cāstiraṅkaḷiṟ
       kaṉavilum
               kāṇappaṭāta viṣayaṅkaḷ anantaṅ kōṭi yirukkiṉṟaṉa,
       hariharā!—
               atirukkaṭṭum vāruṅkaḷ--, iṅkē muṉpōl-- oru poḻutum, īcaṉ
               karuṇaiyiṉmī tāṇaippaṭi, viparītamākavum āccariyakaramāyum
       nāṉ evvitam
               naṭantukoṇṭa pōtilum-- oruvēḷai nāṉ iṉimēl oruvāṟāka
               naṭakkavēṇṭumeṉṟu tīrmāṉittālum tīrmāṉippēṉ.--
       accamayaṅkaḷil
               eṉṉaip pārttu, ivvāṟu kaiyaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu, allatu talaiyai
               metuvāy acaittu, allatu "caritāṉ cari tāṉ teriyum
       eṅkaḷukku" allatu
               "maṉam vaittāl muṭiyum eṅkaḷāl"-- allatu "nāṅkaḷ vāyai
       māttiram
               tiṟappōmāyiṉ"-- allatu "irukkum, appaṭiyiruntāl" ivvāṟāṉa
               cantēkattiṟ kiṭamāṉa vārttaiyaik kūṟi, eṉ viṣayattaippaṟṟi
       nīr ētō
               aṟintavarpōlak kāṭṭikkoḷḷa lākātu. appaṭic ceyvatillaiyeṉṟu
               umatu āpattuk kālattil utavumpaṭi enta īcaṉaip
       pirārttippīrkaḷō
               avarāṇaippaṭi cattiyañceyyuṅkaḷ.
       aru. [kīḻiruntu] cattiyam ceyyuṅkaḷ!
       ha. poṟu poṟu! nimmati yaṟṟa aruvē!
               avarkaḷ cattiyañ ceykiṟārkaḷ.]
               ākavē, tōḻarkaḷē! nāṉ ummītu aṉpu pārāṭṭuvatupōl nīrum
       eṉmītu
               aṉpāyiruppīreṉa nampukiṟēṉ; eḷiya amalātityaṉ taṉṉāl
       umakkeṉṉa
               upakāram ceyyakkūṭumō nēcam pārāṭṭa muṭiyumō, ataṉait
               taṭaiyiṉṟic ceyvāṉeṉa uṟutiyāy nampum. nām oṉṟāyp pōvōm,
               jākkiratai! vāyai māttiram tiṟavātīr! ummai vēṇṭik
       koḷḷukiṟēṉ;
               kālam keṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. ataic carippaṭutta vēṇṭi eṉ
       talaiyil eḻuti
               yirukka vēṇṭumā pāḻākiya eṉ ūḻviṉai! illai vāruṅkaḷ,
       ellōrum
               pōvōm oṉṟāy.
       (pōkiṟārkaḷ.)
       kāṭci muṭikiṟatu.
